L1702: a. The criterion used to determine if a guinea pig had aborted was the delivery of one or more conceptuses or placentas. Several of the abortions were preceded by the presence of a red substance in the nesting box and/or a red perivaginal substance the day before or on the day of the incident. Aborted fetuses and concepti *in utero* were examined to the fullest extent possible using the same methods described for Caesarean-sectioned fetuses (See Section 2.8.6 for details).

b. Satellite - evaluated for progesterone levels.

c. Death occurred five minutes after administration of the fourth dose - death not related to test article.

d. One death occurred approximately 10 minutes after dosing - death not related to test article.

e. One death occurred during the dosing procedure - death not related to test article.

** Significantly different from the Group I value (p&lt;0.01).

** Significantly different from the Group II value (p&lt;0.01).
L1703: The two abortions in the 10 mcg/kg/day dosage group occurred after 14 doses. These sows were gaining weight and had no adverse clinical observations. Deaths and/or moribund sacrifices in the 25 mcg/kg/day group occurred after 5 to 11 doses; abortions occurred in this group after 8 to 14 dosages. Both the deaths and abortions in the 25 mcg/kg/day dosage group were preceded by adverse clinical observations and reductions in body weight. Based on the above information, the abortions and deaths in the 0 (Vehicle), 1 and 10 mcg/kg/day dosage groups were presumed to be the result of trauma from the dosing or a spontaneous event (naturally occurring abortion) and not related to administration of RU-0211. Seven of the nine deaths, moribund sacrifices and abortions in the 25 mcg/kg/day dosage group were presumably related to RU-0211 administration because they occurred in the highest dose group in the presence of maternal toxicity.
L1704: The 25 mcg/kg/day dosages of RU-0211 was associated with adverse clinical signs including ungroomed coat, localized alopecia (particularly on the underside), cold to touch, red perivaginal substance and decreased motor activity (subjective observation, based on sow not attempting to move away or toward the observer). These clinical signs, which were observed in guinea pigs that were found dead or were sacrificed or aborted, occurred at a significantly increased incidences compared to the untreated and vehicle control group values. The observation of red substance in the nesting box or cage pan and/or red perivaginal substance was observed in three of five animals that aborted and was considered to be associated with or indicative of an impending abortion of the litter. The 10 mcg/kg/day dosage was not associated with any of the above adverse clinical signs.
L1705: Additional clinical observations noted in guinea pigs that were found dead, sacrificed or aborted in the 25 mcg/kg/day dosage group included tremors (subjective observation, shaking of the body while in place) red perioral substance, lacrimation, apparent dehydration (decreased skin turgor) scant feces, an abrasion on the mouth (likely due to dosing procedure), excess salivation, edema and gasping. Gasping was observed in one animal (3038) immediately following dosing indicating a problem in dosing.
L1706: The 1, 10 and 25 mcg/kg/day dosages of RU-0211 caused dosage-dependent reductions or significant reductions in body weight gains for the entire dosage and gestation periods. Body weight gains in the 0 (Vehicle), 1, 10 and 25 mcg/kg/day dosage groups were 100.0%, 78.9%, 78.9% and 47.3%, respectively, of the untreated control group values during the dosage period. During the postdosage period, body weight gains for sows in all RU-0211 dosage groups remained reduced by 11.1% to 33.3%. These effects of RU-0211 on body weight gains resulted in significantly reduced body weights in the 25 mcg/kg/day dosage group beginning on DG 42 and continuing until scheduled sacrifice. There were no gross lesions considered related to RU-0211.
L1707: There were 17 to 23 pregnant guinea pigs in each dosage group. Reflecting the mortality and abortion pattern, observations from Caesarean-sections on DG 60 were based on 21, 16, 19, 19 and 14 pregnant sows in the five respective groups with one or more live fetuses, respectively. There were no significant differences between treatment groups and the untreated or treated control groups in litter size, number of live or dead fetuses, or numbers of resorptions.
L1708: Two fetuses in the 25 mcg/kg/day dosage group had multiple alterations noted at gross external examination; one of these two fetuses was dead. The findings were not statistically significant and therefore determined to be unrelated to the administration of RU-0211.
L1709: The progesterone data did not demonstrate any statistically significant differences between groups and there were no detectable trends probably due to the small number of pregnant sows evaluated and the significant variability within groups.

L1728: ## APPENDIX 3
L1729: ## Study SPI/SR05-001: A Study to Evaluate the Abortifacient Effect of RU-0211 in Rhesus Monkeys by Oral Gavage Administration (Sponsor's Executive Summary)
L1730: ## Study Design
L1731: Pregnant animals were assigned to dose groups including vehicle control group. Dose levels were 0 (vehicle control), 10, and 30 µg/kg/day (Table 1).
L1732: **Table 1 Study Groups**

3 / yellow	RU-0211	30	300	0.1	11 (10201 to 10211)
L1734: ## Justification for Selection of the Dose Levels
L1735: The dose levels selected for the current study were based upon the previously completed reproduction studies in rats and the published literature. In the rat studies, the NOEL was determined to be 0.2 mg test article/kg body weight/day. In addition, published data indicates that rhesus monkeys are 3 to 20 times more sensitive than rats to the abortifacient effects of a class of compounds that are similar to the test article3. Therefore, it was believed that the doses selected for the current study in rhesus monkeys (10 and 30 µg/kg/day) allowed for the abortifacient effect of test article to be determined without overt maternal toxicity. In addition, these doses were approximately 4X and 12X the proposed clinical doses (based upon mg/m2).
L1736: ## RESULTS
L1737: ## A. Pregnant Non-Human Primates
L1738: ## 1. Clinical Observations (Tables 1-1 and 1-2)
L1739: No females died, and no abnormalities were observed in clinical signs in any group. Early delivery on Day 149 of gestation was observed in 1 animal (No. 10205) in the 30 µg/kg group and in 1 animal (No. 10107) in the 10 µg/kg group. An abortion on Day 141 of gestation was observed in 1 animal (No. 10104) in the 10 µg/kg group. However, no significant differences were noted in these incidences between the control and test article groups.
L1740: ## 2. Food Consumption (Table 2)
L1741: No significant differences were noted in food consumption between the control and test article groups.
L1742: ## 3. Body Weight (Table 3)
L1743: No significant differences were noted in body weight between the control and test article groups.
L1744: ## 4. Serum Progesterone Concentrations (Table 4)
L1745: No significant differences were noted in serum progesterone concentrations between the control and test article groups.
L1746: ## B. Premature Delivery
L1747: Early delivery on Day 149 of gestation was observed in 1 animal (No. 10205) in the 30 µg/kg group and in 1 animal (No. 10107) in the 10 µg/kg group. These neonates were delivered naturally and alive. No lactational abnormalities were observed in these monkeys. No abnormalities were observed in external features or general health condition in the neonates, which were female and male in the 30 and 10 µg/kg groups, respectively. The body weights of these neonates were 337g and 413g in the 30 and 10 µg/kg groups, respectively.

L1751: ## C. Fetuses
L1752: ## 1. External Examination, Body and Placental Weights (Tables 5-1 and 5-2)
L1753: No external abnormalities were observed in any fetus. No significant differences were noted in fetal body weight or placental weight between the control and test article groups. Fused placenta and single placenta were observed in the 10 µg/kg group, however, no significant differences were noted in their incidences when compared with the control group.
L1754: ## DISCUSSION
L1755: RU-0211 was administered by oral gavage (nasogastric) to 10 or 11 pregnant rhesus monkeys in each group at dose levels of 0 (medium chain fatty acid triglyceride (MCT)), 10, and 30 µg/kg/day during the late gestation period (from Days 110 to 130 of gestation, total: 21 days), in order to evaluate the abortifacient effects on non-human primates.
L1756: No monkeys died, and no abnormalities were observed in clinical signs, food consumption, body weight, or serum progesterone concentrations in any group. Early delivery on Day 149 of gestation was observed in 1 animal in the 30 µg/kg group and in 1 animal in the 10 µg/kg group. The neonates were delivered naturally and alive. No lactational abnormalities were observed in these monkeys. No abnormalities were observed in body
L1757: weight, external features or general health condition in these 2 neonates. Historical control data in SNBL DSR® has shown natural deliveries between GD 144 to 171. Therefore, these natural deliveries on GD 149 were within the historical control range and considered normal. An abortion on Day 141 of gestation was observed in 1 monkey in the 10 µg/kg group. However, this was considered incidental, because the incidence of this premature delivery (before day 150 of gestation) was within the range of the historical control data (mean: 12.9%)® and no abortions occurred in the 30 µg/kg group.
L1758: No test article-related changes were noted in fetal external examinations, fetal body weights, placental examinations, or placental weights in the 30 or 10 µg/kg groups.
L1759: Under the conditions of this study, it was concluded that RU-0211 was not an abortifacient.

L1766: Dose response curve showing Percent Maximal contraction versus Concentration (M) for PGE2, PGE1, PGF2alpha, Misoprostol, and RU-0211. L1767: Figure 2 is a dose response curve showing the inhibition of electrically stimulated twitch contractions in the circular muscle layer of isolated segments of guinea pig ileum. Five compounds are tested: PGE_2 (n=5), PGE_1 (n=3), PGF_{2\alpha} (n=3), Misoprostol, and RU-0211. L1782: Mean Body Weights
Male
Graph showing Mean Body Weights (Y-axis: Grams, 200 to 650) versus Weeks (X-axis: 0 to 120) for Male subjects across four dosage groups (0 UG/KG/DAY, 20 UG/KG/DAY, 100 UG/KG/DAY, 400 UG/KG/DAY). All groups show a similar growth curve, peaking around Week 60-70 at approximately 600-620 grams, followed by a slight decline.
L1783: Mean Body Weights
Female
Graph showing Mean Body Weights (Grains) versus Weeks (0 to 120) for Female animals across four dosage groups: 0 UG/KG/DAY, 20 UG/KG/DAY, 100 UG/KG/DAY, and 400 UG/KG/DAY. All groups show a similar growth curve, reaching approximately 400-420 Grains by Week 100.
L1784: Line graph titled 'Mean Maternal Body Weight' showing body weight (g/animal) versus Gestation Day (0 to 20). Four groups are plotted: 0 mg/kg/day (Control), 0.02 mg/kg/day, 0.2 mg/kg/day, and 2 mg/kg/day. The high dose group (2 mg/kg/day) shows a significantly lower mean body weight compared to controls and lower dose groups, especially after gestation day 10.
L1785: Line graph showing Maternal Body Weights (Replicates A and B Combined) over the Day of Gestation (0 to 60). The Y-axis represents Weight (G) from 0.90 to 1.35. The X-axis represents Day of Gestation from 0 to 60.
The graph displays five groups: Untreated, 0 (Vehicle) MCG/KG/DAY, 1 MCG/KG/DAY, 10 MCG/KG/DAY, and 25 MCG/KG/DAY. All groups show a general increase in weight over gestation, with the Untreated and 0 (Vehicle) groups maintaining the highest weights.
Significance markers are present on the lines, indicating statistical differences between groups at various time points:
*: Significantly different from the Group 1 value (p \le 0.05).
**: Significantly different from the Group 1 value (p \le 0.01).
# : Significantly different from the Group 2 value (p \le 0.05).
###: Significantly different from the Group 2 value (p \le 0.01).
L1786: Dose response curve showing Percent Maximal Contraction (Y-axis, 0 to 140%) versus Concentration (M) (X-axis, logarithmic scale from 10^-9 to 10^-5). Four compounds are plotted: PG_E2 (n=5), PG_D2 (n=4), PGF2alpha (n=4), Misoprostol (n=3), and RU-0211 (n=4). PG_E2 shows the highest maximal response (100% at 10^-5 M). PG_D2 and PGF2alpha show intermediate responses. Misoprostol and RU-0211 show the lowest responses, with RU-0211 reaching approximately 54% maximal contraction at 10^-5 M.
L1787: Dose response curve showing Percent Twitch Inhibition (%) versus Concentration (M) for various compounds. The compounds tested are PGE2 (n=4), PGE3 (n=4), PGF1\alpha (n=3), Misoprostol (n=4), and RU-0211 (n=4). PGE2 shows the highest inhibition, reaching 100% at 10^-2 M. Misoprostol and RU-0211 show moderate inhibition, reaching about 90% at 10^-2 M. PGF1\alpha shows minimal inhibition, reaching about 10% at 10^-2 M.
L1788: Dose response curve showing Percent Maximal Contraction (%) versus Concentration (M) for four compounds: PGF2alpha, PGE2, Misoprostol, and RU-0211. PGF2alpha shows the highest maximal contraction (around 100% at 10^-7 M). PGE2 shows increasing contraction up to 100% at 10^-5 M. Misoprostol and RU-0211 show low maximal contractions (around 20-30%).
L1789: Line graph showing Maternal Body Weights (g) versus Day of Gestation (0 to 60) for five groups: Untreated, 0 (Vehicle) MCG/KG/DAY, 1 MCG/KG/DAY, 10 MCG/KG/DAY, and 25 MCG/KG/DAY. The Untreated group shows the highest weight gain, starting around 0.95g and reaching approximately 1.25g by Day 60. The 0 (Vehicle) group shows slightly lower weight gain, starting around 0.95g and reaching approximately 1.20g. The 1, 10, and 25 MCG/KG/DAY groups show progressively lower weight gain, starting around 0.95g and reaching approximately 1.10g, 1.05g, and 1.00g, respectively, by Day 60. Statistical significance markers are present along the lines, indicating differences between groups at various time points.

L1790: Line graph showing Maternal Body Weight (g) versus Day of Gestation (0 to 60) for Replicate A. The Y-axis ranges from 0.90 to 1.35 g. Six groups are compared: Untreated, 0 (Vehicle) MCG/KG/DAY, 1 MCG/KG/DAY, 10 MCG/KG/DAY, and 25 MCG/KG/DAY. The Untreated group shows the highest weight gain, starting around 1.05 g and reaching approximately 1.30 g by Day 60. The 0 (Vehicle) group shows the lowest weight gain, starting around 1.00 g and reaching approximately 1.15 g. The 1, 10, and 25 MCG/KG/DAY groups show intermediate weight gains, generally tracking between the Untreated and 0 (Vehicle) groups. Statistical significance markers are present, indicating differences from the Group 1 value (Untreated).

L1791: Line graph showing Maternal Body Weight (g) versus Day of Gestation (0 to 60) for Replicate B. The Y-axis ranges from 0.90 to 1.35 g. Six groups are compared: Untreated, 0 (Vehicle) MCG/KG/DAY, 1 MCG/KG/DAY, 10 MCG/KG/DAY, and 25 MCG/KG/DAY. The Untreated group shows the highest weight gain, starting around 1.05 g and reaching approximately 1.30 g by Day 60. The 0 (Vehicle) group shows the lowest weight gain, starting around 1.00 g and reaching approximately 1.15 g. The 1, 10, and 25 MCG/KG/DAY groups show intermediate weight gains, generally tracking between the Untreated and 0 (Vehicle) groups. Statistical significance markers are present, indicating differences from the Group 1 value (Untreated) and Group 2 value (0 (Vehicle)).
